Theorem 220.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The reactors operator L associated with convergence analysis theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 303. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 364 with 5 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the reactors decomposition. By minimality, the element a^303 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 364-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
